# Proof-Point Registry

Every reusable numeric claim swept from the content-bank fragments, clustered so that one underlying fact carries one stable ID.

| Metric | Count |
| --- | ---: |
| Raw claims swept | 2179 |
| Proof-point IDs | 1905 |
| Conflicts to resolve | 32 |
| Consistent (multi-observation) | 149 |
| Single-source | 1724 |
| Distinct blocks referenced | 420 |

`owner` is unassigned and `approved_for_external_use` is `pending` for every ID; both are for the proposal team to fill in.
